We propose a single-encoder framework combining dual-level decorrelation with prototype-guided cross-group contrastive learning to reduce demographic dependence while accounting for within-class variation. We further propose Demographic Representation Alignment Reduction (DRAR), a new metric that quantifies the reduction in demographic structure within disease representations. The framework is evaluated on four classification tasks using 34,809 CheXpert test images across eight intersectional groups, defined by age, sex and ethnicity. Compared with empirical risk minimization (ERM), our method reduces the mean equalized-odds gap from 15.41\% to 10.86\% and the AUC gap from 5.95\% to 5.01\%. Our method achieves a DRAR of 59.04\% relative to ERM, with only a slight decrease in mean AUC. These results demonstrate that representation disentanglement can reduce demographic bias and improve intersectional fairness. Code is available at \url{https://github.com/06Yujie/Fair-Medical-Imaging}.

</details>


### [178] [Local2Mesh: Spatially Localized Contour-to-Mesh for Left Ventricular Reconstruction from Sparse 2D Cardiac MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06052)
*Haoyu Wu,Ling Lin,Pascal Lefèvre,Ruizhe Li,Xiaowu Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Local2Mesh框架，从稀疏2D轮廓重建3D左心室网格，通过几何感知对齐和局部路由机制提升重建与下游分析效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏CMR图像重建3D左心室面临层间错位和层间局部空间信息不足的问题，且全局轮廓特征聚合会掩盖局部轮廓到表面的关系。

Method: 提出Local2Mesh，一个空间局部化的轮廓到网格框架，将模板网格变形以重建3D左心室几何，无需3D网格标注。引入几何感知对齐纠正层间错位，以及平面感知Local Router，基于顶点到平面的距离将轮廓特征路由到模板顶点，局部与全局轮廓特征共同指导基于图的模板变形。

Result: 在M&Ms-2和ACDC两个公开数据集上，几何重建和功能估计均优于现有方法；从M&Ms-2到ACDC的零样本迁移展现强跨数据集泛化能力；重建网格比稀疏轮廓更能提升疾病分类效果。

Conclusion: 结合几何感知对齐与局部轮廓到顶点建模可改善稀疏2D轮廓的左心室重建，并支持下游心脏分析。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) left ventricular (LV) reconstruction from sparse cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging remains challenging due to inter-slice misalignment and insufficient local spatial information between slices. Global aggregation of contour features may obscure local contour-to-surface relationships. We propose Local2Mesh, a spatially localized contour-to-mesh framework that deforms a template mesh to reconstruct 3D LV geometry from sparse 2D contours without 3D mesh annotations. The framework introduces geometry-aware alignment to correct inter-slice misalignment and a plane-aware Local Router that routes contour features to template vertices using vertex-to-plane distances. Local and global contour features then jointly guide graph-based template deformation for 3D LV reconstruction. Experiments on two public datasets, M\&Ms-2 and ACDC, demonstrate superior geometric reconstruction and functional estimation over existing methods. Zero-shot transfer from M\&Ms-2 to ACDC demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization. Reconstructed meshes also improve disease classification over sparse contours, supporting their utility for downstream cardiac analysis. These results demonstrate that combining geometry-aware alignment with local contour-to-vertex modeling improves LV reconstruction from sparse 2D contours and supports downstream cardiac analysis. The code is available at \url{https://github.com/hwu918945-alt/loca2mesh}.

</details>


### [179] [ROT: Rotating Hidden States towards Contextual Vectors for Hallucination Mitigation in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06056)
*Yijing Du,Xiangcheng Zhan,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROT是一个无需训练、层级特定的框架，通过检测中间层隐藏状态的语义偏差并施加保范旋转，将隐藏状态校正回多模态上下文平面，从而减少LVLM的对象幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）常出现对象幻觉。现有无需训练的干预方法主要操纵注意力权重，只能间接影响到达最终预测层的深层语义，效果有限。作者希望从更直接的层面入手。

Method: 作者聚焦于自注意力与残差相加后提取的隐藏状态向量。经验分析发现，幻觉token并非单纯过度依赖语言先验，而是在中间层表现出异常的上下文偏差——与文本和视觉上下文的相似度都显著偏低。基于此提出ROT：在中间层动态检测语义偏差，施加保范旋转（norm-preserving rotation），将隐藏状态引导回由上下文张成的局部多模态上下文平面；在后续层引入表征平滑机制以稳定校正后的轨迹。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，ROT在不同模型架构和规模上均能持续减少幻觉。

Conclusion: ROT为有依据的生成提供了一种高效、几何驱动的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) frequently suffer from object hallucination. Existing training-free interventions primarily manipulate attention weights, which indirectly affect the deep semantics reaching the final predictive layers. In this work, we shift our focus to the hidden state vectors extracted after self-attention and residual addition. Empirical analysis reveals that hallucinated tokens do not simply over-rely on linguistic priors; instead, they exhibit an anomalous contextual deviation, showing significantly lower similarities to both textual and visual contexts in intermediate layers. Motivated by this, we propose ROT, a layer-specific, training-free framework. ROT dynamically detects semantic deviation in the middle layers and applies a norm-preserving rotation to steer the hidden states back toward the local multimodal context plane spanned by the contexts. For subsequent layers, a representational smoothing mechanism is introduced to stabilize the calibrated trajectory. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ROT consistently reduces hallucinations across various model architectures and scales, offering an efficient, geometry-driven solution for grounded generation.

</details>


### [180] [Vision Transformer Ensembles for Panoramic Street Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06063)
*Yunus Serhat Bıçakçı*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍PalmCity挑战赛第一名方案，比较九种预训练分割系统，最终用DINOv3编码器+掩码Transformer集成方案取得隐藏测试集57.08% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 街景全景语义分割可支持城市环境描述，但数据集小、训练成本不均，导致模型选择困难。

Method: 在近似算力预算下比较九种预训练分割系统（DeepLabV3+、SegFormer、UPerNet、Mask2Former、DINOv3+线性解码器、仅编码器掩码Transformer等）；对两个领先候选用3个随机种子和更长预算独立训练。

Result: 3个仅编码器掩码Transformer模型的类别概率等权平均，3尺度+水平翻转推理，公开验证集60.95% mIoU、71.16% mF1；隐藏测试集57.08% mIoU、67.96% mF1；249张测试掩码耗时251.49秒，峰值GPU内存2.70 GiB。

Conclusion: 报告所有候选模型、推理变体、类别级误差与复现检查，提供了使用现有架构的完整挑战工作流。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of street panoramas can support detailed descriptions of urban environments, yet small datasets and unequal training costs make model selection difficult. This paper presents the system used for a first place submission to the PalmCity challenge in the leaderboard snapshot dated 5 October 2026. Nine pretrained segmentation systems are compared using approximately equal computation budgets. The candidates include DeepLabV3+, SegFormer, UPerNet, Mask2Former, DINOv3 with a linear decoder, and an Encoder only Mask Transformer using DINOv3. The two leading candidates are trained independently with three random seeds and longer budgets. Equal averaging of class probabilities from the three Encoder only Mask Transformer models, evaluated at three image scales with horizontal reflection, produces 60.95% mean intersection over union and 71.16% mean F1 on the 84 image public validation split. The submitted predictions receive 57.08% mean intersection over union and 67.96% mean F1 on the hidden test leaderboard. Producing all 249 test masks takes 251.49 seconds including model initialization and provenance checks on one NVIDIA RTX 5090. Peak allocated GPU memory is 2.70 GiB. The study reports all eligible models, all inference variants, class level errors, source conditions, and reproducibility checks, providing a documented challenge workflow with existing architectures.

</details>


### [181] [Anatomy-preserving unpaired cone-beam CT refinement for image-guided radiotherapy using pseudo-label guided diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06094)
*Qi Lai,Yutong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RefineCBCT，一种基于伪标签引导和短步扩散的非配对CBCT精修框架，在减少伪影的同时保持患者解剖结构，在LUNG和PELVIC数据集上优于GAN和扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT广泛用于图像引导放疗，但散射、射束硬化、噪声、截断等伪影限制了图像质量和CT值准确性。临床中因运动、解剖变化和采集不匹配，配对CBCT-CT数据难以获取。

Method: RefineCBCT采用非配对学习框架，结合伪标签引导与短步扩散模型来减少伪影并保留患者特异性解剖。在LUNG TCIA和PELVIC TCIA的公开非配对CBCT与计划CT数据上训练和评估，并与代表性GAN和扩散方法对比。

Result: LUNG TCIA上所有指标最优：MAE 19.411、RMSE 62.758、PSNR 30.845 dB、SSIM 0.931。PELVIC TCIA上MAE、PSNR、SSIM最优：14.905、36.671 dB、0.876。精修图像条状和阴影伪影更少，解剖边界更清晰，软组织均匀性提升，线剖面和ROI分析与计划CT更一致。

Conclusion: RefineCBCT在临床真实的非配对训练条件下提供了高效有效的CBCT精修，可支持图像引导放疗流程中更可靠的CBCT应用。代码已在GitHub公开，评估数据集可从TCIA获取。

Abstract: Cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) is widely used in image-guided radiotherapy, but scatter, beam hardening, noise, truncation, and other artifacts limit image quality and CT number accuracy. Paired CBCT and CT data are difficult to obtain clinically because of motion, anatomical changes, and acquisition mismatch. We present RefineCBCT, an unpaired CBCT refinement framework that uses pseudo-label guidance and short-step diffusion to reduce artifacts while preserving patient-specific anatomy. RefineCBCT was trained and evaluated on unpaired CBCT and planning CT data from public LUNG TCIA and PELVIC TCIA datasets and compared with representative GAN and diffusion based methods. On LUNG TCIA, it achieved the best results across all metrics, with MAE 19.411, RMSE 62.758, PSNR 30.845 dB, and SSIM 0.931. On PELVIC TCIA, it achieved the best MAE, PSNR, and SSIM, with values of 14.905, 36.671 dB, and 0.876. The refined images showed fewer streaking and shading artifacts, clearer anatomical boundaries, and improved soft tissue uniformity, with line profile and ROI analyses showing closer agreement with planning CT. These results suggest that RefineCBCT provides efficient and effective CBCT refinement under clinically realistic unpaired training conditions and may support more reliable CBCT use in image-guided radiotherapy workflows. Code is publicly available on GitHub, and the evaluated datasets are available from The Cancer Imaging Archive.

</details>


### [182] [Benchmarking CLIP for Zero-Shot Face and Periocular Gender Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06102)
*Fernando Alonso-Fernandez,Kevin Hernandez-Diaz,Jose Maria Buades,Josef Bigun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究CLIP在零样本性别估计中的应用，完整人脸达95.54%，眼周区域因偏置问题略低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索CLIP在零样本性别估计（尤其是更具挑战性的眼周图像）上的能力。

Method: 评估三种CLIP主干，用男/女文本提示的图像-文本相似度；对眼周进行阈值对齐；并用CLIP特征训练线性SVM。

Result: 完整人脸零样本准确率95.54%；眼周原始预测偏向男性，阈值对齐后达85.29%；线性SVM眼周最佳86.17%，比以往Adience结果高约2.8%。

Conclusion: CLIP零样本在完整人脸上表现优异；眼周性别估计更难，偏置可通过阈值对齐缓解。

Abstract: We investigate CLIP for zero-shot gender estimation from full-face and periocular images. Three CLIP backbones are evaluated on 11,299 frontal images from Adience using image-text similarity with male/female prompts, achieving 95.54% full-face accuracy without task-specific training. For periocular, zero-shot predictions are strongly biased towards males, primarily due to a misaligned decision boundary. Threshold alignment substantially reduces this bias, reaching 85.29% accuracy. Linear SVMs trained on CLIP features provide only marginal gains, with a best periocular accuracy of 86.17%, approximately 2.8% above previous Adience results in the literature. Nevertheless, the gap with full-face performance confirms the greater difficulty of periocular gender estimation

</details>


### [183] [AnchorGen: Anchored Optimization for Customizable Generative 3D Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06135)
*Hantao Zhang,Oliver Heinimann,Jieke Wu,Yingxuan You,Emilien Seiler,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AnchorGen，一种在形状-草图联合潜空间上无条件训练的整流流框架，通过推理时约束草图分量，实现保设计风格的 3D 形状优化、维度编辑与纯草图合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习生成先验的 3D 形状优化对 2D 草图不敏感：优化漂移后，先验只将方案拉回训练分布的高密度区，而忽略用户指定的设计风格与比例。

Method: 在配对数据的形状与草图拼接潜变量上无条件训练整流流模型，学习二者联合分布；推理时冻结草图约束，用梯度下降优化形状潜变量以最小化可微阻力代理，并通过 token 级余弦惩罚使草图潜变量贴近目标草图。

Result: 单一模型同时支持三类任务：保设计优化、显式维度编辑、以及仅凭草图的形状合成。

Conclusion: 将条件性从训练转移到推理，用联合流形上的草图约束替代显式条件，可在保证几何有效的同时严格遵循草图指定的设计意图。

Abstract: Engineering design often starts from a 2D sketch that fixes style and proportions, yet the subsequent 3D shape optimization relies on learned generative priors to keep the geometry valid. However, these priors are agnostic to the sketch: while they admit a valid design by correcting a drifted proposal back to its training distribution, they often correct it towards the high-density region, ignoring the specified design. We introduce \emph{AnchorGen}, a rectified-flow framework trained unconditionally on the concatenated shape and sketch latents of paired data. The learned manifold represents the joint distribution of shape-sketch pairs, so constraining the sketch component restricts the iterate to the sub-manifold of shapes consistent with a target style. Since training employs no conditioning signal, the constraint is imposed at inference: gradient descent optimizes the shape latent to minimize a differentiable drag surrogate, while constraining the sketch latent to remain close to the target sketch via a token-wise cosine penalty. A single model thereby supports design-preserving optimization, dimensionally explicit design edits, and sketch-only synthesis.

</details>


### [184] [Efficient Test-time Adaptation through Candidate Verification and Divergence Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06147)
*Seungmin Oh,Seunghun Kang,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Test-Time Correction (TTC)，将VLM的测试时适应重构为候选验证问题，通过“假设-重建-纠正”的无训练框架，在多个TTA设定下提升精度并加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM-TTA方法多采用预测侧适应范式，用测试样本调整logits、原型、缓存等，常带来额外计算开销，且需迭代优化。

Method: 将VLM-TTA重新定义为候选验证。对测试特征和top-k候选标签，将每个候选视为假设，在记忆库对应潜在子空间中重建特征，测量插入测试特征后子空间及其关系的“分歧偏移”。正确候选偏移小，错误候选扰动大，选最小聚合偏移者作为修正预测。

Result: 在5种TTA设定、15个基准数据集（零样本分类、域泛化、少样本分类、base-to-novel泛化、跨数据集评估）上，TTC持续超越SOTA VLM-TTA方法，实现最高2倍加速、CPU内存降低3倍以上、GPU内存降低最高1.4倍。

Conclusion: TTC是一种无训练、无需迭代优化的候选验证机制，在精度与效率间取得良好平衡，为VLM-TTA提供了新视角。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong zero-shot transferability but remain vulnerable to target-domain shifts at inference time. Test-time adaptation (TTA) offers a practical remedy, yet most existing VLM-TTA methods follow a prediction-side adaptation paradigm. They use test samples to adjust logits, prototypes, caches, priors, or feature statistics, often incurring additional computational overhead. In this paper, we take a different perspective and reframe VLM-TTA as candidate verification rather than prediction adjustment. We propose Test-Time Correction (TTC), a hypothesis-based correction framework guided by a simple principle: hypothesize, reconstruct, correct. Given a test feature and its top-k candidate labels, TTC treats each candidate label as a hypothesis, reconstructs the feature within the corresponding latent subspace stored in a memory bank, and measures the resulting divergence shift. This shift quantifies how much the candidate subspace and its relations to other candidates change after the hypothetical insertion of the test feature. A correct candidate hypothesis induces only a small shift, whereas an incorrect one perturbs the subspace more strongly. TTC therefore corrects the prediction by selecting the candidate with the minimum aggregated divergence shift. This training-free candidate-verification mechanism avoids iterative optimization and provides a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across five TTA settings and 15 benchmark datasets, including zero-shot classification, domain generalization, few-shot classification, base-to-novel generalization, and cross-dataset evaluation, TTC consistently improves accuracy over state-of-the-art VLM-TTA methods while achieving up to 2x speedup, over 3x lower CPU memory usage, and up to 1.4x lower GPU memory usage than the lowest-memory training-free baseline.

</details>


### [185] [Bayesian Optimization in Sequence-to-Architecture Latent Space for Zero-Shot NAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06167)
*Ondrej Tybl,Lukas Neumann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将贝叶斯优化引入零样本NAS以支持大规模架构搜索：训练VAE学习架构的前缀编码潜空间，并用代理标量化将多个零样本代理合并为单一BO目标，仅1万次迭代（单GPU 8小时）即找到在分类、检测、分割三项任务上达到SOTA的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本NAS虽免去训练成本，但其基于进化算法的搜索缺乏显式目标建模，常退化为近似随机的变异搜索；贝叶斯优化可建模目标并跨迭代聚合信息，但难以扩展到现代NAS的高维、离散、图结构空间，仅适用于小型网络。

Method: 通过VAE学习潜空间，该VAE被训练来重建架构的一种新型前缀编码；并提出代理标量化方法，将多个零样本代理组合成单一的贝叶斯优化目标，从而在潜空间中对大规模架构进行BO搜索。

Result: 在给定的模型参数量约束下，仅进行10,000次搜索迭代（单GPU约8小时），所找到的架构在图像分类、目标检测、语义分割三项任务上均取得state-of-the-art结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合潜空间学习与代理标量化的贝叶斯优化能有效克服零样本NAS中随机搜索与可扩展性问题，为大规模架构的高效零样本搜索提供了可行且高效的方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot Neural Architecture Search removes the prohibitive cost of traditional NAS, but its search process is typically based on the evolutionary algorithm (EA); lacking an explicit model of the objective, it often resorts to a near-random search through mutation. Bayesian Optimization offers a principled alternative by modeling the objective and aggregating information across iterations, but scales poorly to the high-dimensional, discrete, graph-structured spaces of modern NAS, restricting its use to only small networks. In this paper, we bring Bayesian Optimization to zero-shot NAS for large-scale architectures by learning a latent space via a Variational Autoencoder trained to reconstruct a novel prefix encoding of architectures and propose a proxy scalarization that combines several zero-shot proxies into a single Bayesian Optimization objective. After only 10,000 iterations of the proposed search algorithm (8 hours on a single GPU), our method found a network architecture which under the given model parameter count constraints achieves state-of-the-art results on three separate tasks -- image classification, object detection and semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [186] [EORestore-Agent: Fidelity-Guided Agentic Restoration of Remote Sensing Images with Composite Degradations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06196)
*Heli Qi,Zeqi Zhou,Jingjun Yi,Kunyi Liu,Ziyang Lihe,Junjue Wang,Osamu Yoshie,Naoto Yokoya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EORestore-Agent，将遥感图像复合退化修复建模为可验证的逐步决策轨迹，用无参考的每步评分替代不可测的目标，在合成基准上显著优于一体化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像常同时存在雾、云、噪声、模糊、低光和低分辨率等复合退化，修复时需决定用哪些工具、以何顺序、何时停止，但推理时没有干净的参考图像来验证这些决策。基于单一退化训练的一体化模型在退化叠加下会收敛到狭窄的 PSNR 区间。

Method: EORestore-Agent：微调视觉语言模型报告所有残留退化类型，将其对应的工具池合并打分，使修复顺序由逐步选择自然产生；训练一个相对质量评分器，用合成退化链上的全参考监督，预测从当前图像到各候选结果的 PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS 变化。仅当所有预测变化非负且 PSNR 增益为正时接受该步，否则保留当前图像。

Result: 在含六种退化类型的合成 Landsat-8 基准上，对二到六种退化复合体，EORestore-Agent 比最强重训一体化基线提升 PSNR 2.3–3.2 dB；而零样本自然图像智能体在 18 个设置中有 17 个 PSNR 低于退化输入。用无参考质量差替代学习评分器会损失 1.1–4.6 dB。剩余有害步骤较小且集中在接受阈值附近。Sentinel-2 示例显示无需重训即可迁移到真实大气退化。

Conclusion: 通过将修复目标转化为无参考、可验证的逐步决策，EORestore-Agent 有效应对遥感复合退化，并在合成与真实场景下展现出优于一体化基线的性能与迁移能力。

Abstract: Remote sensing images often carry composite degradations, in which haze, cloud, noise, blur, low light, and low resolution coexist. Restoring them requires deciding which tool to apply, in what order, and when to stop, yet no clean reference is available at inference time to verify these decisions. All-in-one models trained on single degradations converge to a narrow PSNR band as degradations accumulate. To formulate real-world remote sensing restoration as a traceable trajectory, we present EORestore-Agent, which replaces this unmeasurable objective with reference-free, verifiable per-step decisions. A fine-tuned vision-language model reports all residual degradation types, whose tool pools are scored together, so the restoration order emerges from step-wise selection. A relative quality scorer, trained with full-reference supervision on synthetic degradation chains, predicts the changes in PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS from the current image to each candidate. A step is accepted only when no predicted change is negative and the predicted PSNR gain is positive. Otherwise, the agent keeps the current image. On a synthetic Landsat-8 benchmark with six degradation types, EORestore-Agent improves PSNR by 2.3 to 3.2 dB over the strongest retrained all-in-one baseline on composites of two to six degradations, whereas zero-shot natural-image agents fall below the degraded input in PSNR in 17 of 18 settings. Replacing the learned scorer with no-reference quality differences costs 1.1 to 4.6 dB. The remaining harmful steps are small and cluster near the acceptance threshold. Sentinel-2 examples illustrate transfer to real atmospheric degradation without retraining.

</details>


### [187] [MoCAR: Motion-code Coordinate-aware AutoRegression for Continuous Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06210)
*Yiming Xu,Hao Cheng,Monika Sester*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoCAR 将轨迹预测建模为坐标感知连续隐空间中的下一码元预测,用单阶段 decoder-only 架构在 Argoverse 上取得顶级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言的自回归生成天然成立,因为预测的 token 可直接作为下一步状态;但轨迹预测缺乏这种干净的 token——运动是连续的、多模态的,且表达在随预测轨迹演化的局部坐标系中。

Method: 提出 MoCAR(运动码坐标感知自回归),一个 decoder-only 框架,在坐标感知连续隐空间中进行下一码元预测。它从端点归一化的轨迹片段学习连续运动码空间,每个码元同时捕捉局部几何与参考系转换。历史码元作为 teacher-forced 前缀,未来码元在时序、地图、智能体、模式交互下自回归生成;预测码元保留在隐记忆中以供 rollout,解码端点更新局部场景上下文。无需轨迹空间重 token 化、轨迹查询、目标候选或提案-精炼流程。

Result: 在 Argoverse(AV)基准上,MoCAR 以简单单阶段架构达到顶级性能,从 AV2 到 AV1 零样本迁移强,并在转弯密集场景中提升。消融证实学习的连续运动码空间、隐对齐、弱 KL 正则和联合 tokenizer-预测器优化对稳定隐自回归至关重要。

Conclusion: MoCAR 证明轨迹预测可作为坐标感知连续隐空间中的自回归下一码元预测,无需复杂多阶段流程即可取得 SOTA 级效果并具备强泛化性。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation is natural for language, where predicted tokens can be directly reused as the next prediction state, but trajectory forecasting lacks such a clean token: motion is continuous, multimodal, and expressed in local coordinate frames that evolve with the predicted trajectory. We present MoCAR (Motion-code Coordinate-aware AutoRegression), a decoder-only framework that casts trajectory forecasting as next-code prediction in a coordinate-aware continuous latent space. MoCAR learns a continuous motion-code space from endpoint-normalized trajectory segments, where each code jointly captures local trajectory geometry and the reference-frame transition induced by that segment. Historical motion codes are used as a teacher-forced prefix, future codes are generated autoregressively under temporal, map, agent, and mode interactions, and predicted codes persist in latent memory while decoded endpoints update the local scene context. This enables rollout without trajectory-space re-tokenization, trajectory queries, goal candidates, or proposal-and-refinement pipelines. On Argoverse (AV) benchmarks, MoCAR achieves top-tier performance with a simple single-stage architecture, transfers strongly from AV2 to AV1 in zero-shot evaluation, and improves on turn-heavy scenarios. Ablations confirm that the learned continuous motion-code space, latent alignment, weak KL regularization, and joint tokenizer-predictor optimization are essential for stable latent autoregression.

</details>


### [188] [Frequency-Decoupled Diffusion Guidance for Non-Blind Image Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06221)
*Sihan Wang,Jinshu Huang,Haibin Su,Yunhua Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出频率解耦后验引导方法,将频率激活与衰减感知的频谱正则化分离,用于无训练扩散模型图像复原,并引入局部轨迹正则化器稳定采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频率感知方法未显式区分频率激活与退化引起的衰减,导致非激活频率间的衰减差异建模不足。

Method: 渐进低频到高频调度确定活跃测量频带;由核导出的衰减图定义非活跃分量的选择性频谱先验;引入局部轨迹正则化器抑制空间不规则的偏离;通过Tweedie插件近似进行局部能量校正构建时间相关引导,并给出KL正则化的路径空间解释。

Result: 在自然图像基准的非盲去模糊任务上,即使测量噪声较高,PSNR与SSIM表现仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 频率解耦后验引导有效提升了无训练扩散复原的鲁棒性与重建质量。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models provide powerful image priors for training-free posterior sampling in image restoration. To guide this sampling process, frequency-aware methods progressively incorporate measurement information across frequency bands, facilitating coarse-to-fine reconstruction. However, existing methods typically do not explicitly separate frequency activation from degradation-induced attenuation, leaving attenuation differences among inactive frequencies insufficiently modeled. In this work, we propose frequency-decoupled posterior guidance to separate frequency activation from attenuation-aware spectral regularization. Specifically, a progressive low-to-high frequency schedule determines the active measurement band, while a kernel-derived attenuation map defines a selective spectral prior over inactive components. To stabilize the sampling process, we also introduce a local trajectory regularizer that suppresses spatially irregular state-to-clean deviations. For a fixed endpoint energy, we provide a KL-regularized path-space interpretation. In practice, we construct time-dependent guidance through local energy corrections using a Tweedie plug-in approximation. Experiments on natural-image benchmarks demonstrate strong PSNR and SSIM performance across challenging non-blind deblurring settings, even at higher measurement noise levels.

</details>


### [189] [Joint Class-Time Learning for Video Classification with Multi-Instance Partial-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06234)
*Lingyu Shen,Wei Tang,Fakhri Karray,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PIVOTMIPL，通过联合类别-时间分配把标签消歧与时间证据分配耦合，解决多实例偏标签学习在视频分类中包级标签无法监督候选类与时间证据对应的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多实例偏标签学习同时存在实例空间与标签空间的不精确监督，可用于视频分类；但包级标签无法显式监督候选类别与时间证据之间的对应关系，需要一种能联合完成标签消歧与时间证据分配的方法。

Method: 采用联合 class-time assignment：占用正则化球面匹配关联上下文化视频特征，学习非均匀时间质量和抑制过度集中；训练中用候选受限推理在候选标签集内重算分配；双边缘 KL 投影构建结构化教师，融合动量精炼的类别信念并保留提案时间占用；用单一 plan-level KL 目标对齐全空间预测器。理论分析刻画候选重解与 masking 的差异，并证明联合目标可分解为类别边缘监督与类别条件时间监督。

Result: 基于 Breakfast、DoTA、FineAction 用模型生成候选标签构建 VCMIPL 基准，在四种特征表示上评估；实验表明 PIVOTMIPL 在有效性和效率上均优于现有 MIPL 算法。

Conclusion: PIVOTMIPL 通过联合类别-时间分配有效应对 MIPL 视频分类中的不精确监督，并为候选重解与结构化教师监督提供理论解释，在多个基准与特征表示上取得更好效果与效率。

Abstract: Multi-instance partial-label learning (MIPL) addresses inexact supervision in both the instance and label spaces, which can be applied to video classification. However, bag-level labels do not explicitly supervise the correspondence between candidate classes and temporal evidence. We propose {\ours}, which couples label disambiguation with temporal evidence allocation through a joint class--time assignment. Occupancy-regularized spherical matching associates contextualized video features while learning nonuniform temporal mass and discouraging excessive concentration. During training, candidate-restricted inference recomputes the assignment within the candidate label set. A dual-marginal KL projection then constructs a structured teacher that incorporates momentum-refined class beliefs while preserving the proposal's temporal occupancy. A single plan-level KL objective aligns the full-space predictor with this teacher. Our analysis characterizes when candidate re-solving differs from masking and shows that, under the stated construction, the joint objective decomposes into class-marginal and class-conditional temporal supervision. We construct VCMIPL benchmarks from Breakfast, DoTA, and FineAction using model-generated candidate labels and evaluate the method across four feature representations. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that PIVOTMIPL outperforms existing MIPL algorithms in both effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [190] [CentriQ: Calibration-Free Quantization of Diffusion Transformers via Exact Mean Centering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06260)
*Nataša Jovanović,Mathieu Salzmann,Saqib Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CentriQ：免校准的4-bit DiT量化方法，通过中心化与秩-1分支解决Hadamard旋转无法均匀分散均值的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT采样成本高，4-bit量化可降本，但现有方法不足：校准法依赖特定checkpoint与prompt分布；免数据Hadamard旋转在LLM有效却在DiT上掉质量。

Method: 发现结构性问题：自适应layer-norm条件会给激活加逐token均值，在评估DiT宽度下Hadamard旋转无法均匀分散该均值，单一主方向存留并决定量化范围。CentriQ在旋转前对每个token中心化，并用rank-1全精度分支精确还原均值，从而无需数据即可闭式得到逐token scale；权重用鲁棒ℓp目标拟合，跟踪每组密集模式并抑制重尾。

Result: 在三个DiT上，CentriQ在4-bit匹配校准SVDQuant质量；而带朴素逐token激活量化的免校准权重量化方法崩溃或显著退化。2-bit权重下超过最强免校准方法，并首个在2-bit激活下保持可用图像质量。

Conclusion: CentriQ是不依赖校准的DiT量化器，解决了Hadamard旋转的均值分散结构问题，在4-bit/2-bit均达成领先的免校准质量。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve state-of-the-art image generation, but their sampling cost limits deployment. Quantizing both weights and activations to 4 bits reduces this cost, yet existing methods fall short in one of two ways. Calibration-based methods are tied to a specific checkpoint and prompt distribution, whereas data-free Hadamard rotation, effective for LLMs, loses quality on DiTs. We show that this loss has a structural cause. Adaptive layer-norm conditioning adds a per-token mean to the activations, and at the widths of the evaluated DiTs, the Hadamard rotations used by data-free methods cannot spread this mean uniformly across coordinates. A single dominant direction therefore survives the rotation and sets the quantization range. We introduce CentriQ, a calibration-free quantizer that centers each token before rotation and restores the mean exactly through a rank-1 full-precision branch, so that per-token scales follow in closed form without data. Weights are fitted under a robust $\ell_p$ objective that tracks the dense mode of each group and discounts heavy tails. Across three DiTs, CentriQ matches the quality of calibrated SVDQuant at 4 bits, whereas calibration-free weight quantizers with plain per-token activation quantization collapse or degrade substantially. CentriQ outperforms the strongest calibration-free method reported to date at 2-bit weights. It is also the first calibration-free method to retain usable image quality at 2-bit activations.

</details>


### [191] [VepAgent: Bridging Causal-Transition via Tool-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Video Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06293)
*Qiutong Chen,Yuchan Guo,Zhenlong Yuan,Haobo Yang,Fangfang Lin,Xinyi Long,Yin Wang,Zijian Song,Rui Lan,Shi Qiu,Boyuan Pan,Yang Luo,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VepAgent，一个整合因果过渡推理与工具增强强化学习的智能体框架用于视频事件预测，并构建FutureBench-4K数据集，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在视频事件预测中依赖回顾式摘要和文本中心先验，难以桥接未观测的因果过渡，导致从观测状态到未来事件的推理能力受限。

Method: 1) 构建FutureBench-4K高质量思维链数据集用于SFT，衔接因果逻辑缺口；2) 开发包含状态跟踪、帧检索、区域放大的诊断工具库，支持推理时动态增强；3) 提出联合优化预测准确率、因果一致性和可靠先验的复合奖励机制，驱动智能体基于真实视觉证据而非文本相似性。

Result: 在FutureBench和NEPBench数据集上取得SOTA性能，显著超越更大规模的MLLM。

Conclusion: 验证了智能体式、面向未来的推理范式的有效性，为VEP提供了显式建模因果过渡的新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable potential in video understanding, yet their reliance on retrospective summarization and text-centric priors often limits their ability to bridge unobserved causal transitions when applied to Video Event Prediction (VEP). To address this, we propose VepAgent, an agentic framework that integrates causal-transition reasoning with tool-augmented reinforcement learning (RL) for robust VEP. Unlike prior methods that passively project future trajectories from historical dependencies, our approach explicitly models the logical progression from terminal observed states to future events. Specifically, we first construct futurebench-4K, a high-quality chain-of-thought dataset for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) that effectively bridges the causal-logic gap by structuring the deduction of unobserved intermediate states. Subsequently, we develop a diagnostic tool library integrating state tracking, frame retrieval, and region magnification, enabling the agent to dynamically augment reasoning with external tools to recover missing spatio-temporal evidence and resolve visual ambiguities during inference. Moreover, we propose a composite reward mechanism that jointly optimizes prediction accuracy, causal coherence, and reliable prior, compelling the agent to rely on genuine visual grounding rather than superficial textual similarities. Extensive evaluations on FutureBench and NEPBench datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming larger MLLMs and validating the empirical effectiveness of our agentic, future-oriented reasoning paradigm.

</details>


### [192] [Wiring Matters: Injection Topology and Initialization of Affordance Heads in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06318)
*Zijian An,Linhan Wang,Jiayan Wang,Shijie Geng,Ran Yang,Yiming Feng,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在LIBERO上系统研究了如何将密集可供性(affordance)监督接入现代VLA策略而不损害指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集可供性监督是有吸引力的辅助信号，但朴素地共训练一个affordance头会严重破坏指令跟随。作者希望找出正确的"接线"方式。

Method: 通过stop-gradient读取backbone，并用学习到的bridge将中间头特征重注入action expert。做了2×2消融：注入拓扑（concatenation vs residual）× bridge初始化（zero vs random），并加两个探针实验。

Result: stop-gradient是先决条件：允许affordance梯度回传backbone会使策略低于无头基线（85.5% vs 93.1%）。初始化是主导因素。最佳接法（主动初始化的residual bridge）达96.2%，匹配更复杂的AffordanceVLA（95.8%），仅增加不到1%参数。

Conclusion: 保护backbone后，bridge初始化是关键；主动bridge成为负载承载部件，而lazy bridge仅作为训练时正则化器。真值affordance作为输入反而有害。

Abstract: Dense affordance supervision is an appealing auxiliary signal for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, yet naively co-training an affordance head can severely damage instruction following. We present a controlled study of how to wire such a head into a modern VLA on the LIBERO benchmark. Our recipe reads the backbone through a stop-gradient and re-injects an intermediate head feature into the action expert via a learned bridge. The stop-gradient is a precondition: letting affordance gradients reach the backbone drops the policy below the headless base (85.5% vs. 93.1%). With the backbone protected, a same-budget 2*2 ablation over injection topology (concatenation vs. residual) and bridge initialization (zero vs. random) shows initialization is the dominant lever. The best wiring, an actively initialized residual bridge, reaches 96.2%, matching the far more elaborate three-expert AffordanceVLA (95.8%) with under 1% extra parameters. Two probes explain the mechanism: ground-truth affordances fed as an input hurt, and inference-time zeroing shows a lazy bridge acts only as a training-time regularizer while an active bridge becomes load-bearing.

</details>


### [193] [Readout Blindness: VLM Scores Miss the Spatial Direction Their Frozen Encoders Retain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06324)
*Guangyuan Li,Tianming Du,Yan Jiang,Bihan Wen,Jiancheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIP类模型在方向性空间关系上近乎随机，作者提出"readout blindness"理论解释（对称评分规则会抵消方向），并提出无需训练的ADR方法恢复方向信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP类视觉-语言模型在多模态系统中很重要，但在"左/右"等有向空间关系上得分接近随机。作者想从理论和实证上解释为何已部署评分规则会丢失方向信息。

Method: 理论分析表明对称评分规则会使方向抵消，与编码器训练无关。提出Antisymmetric Displacement Readout (ADR)：在冻结特征中将caption词与图像patch对齐，用匹配到的物体质心之间的有符号位移来评分关系，无需额外训练或参数。并引入prior deflation，用"配错图像"作为null基线来衡量配对带来的grounded gain。

Result: 跨多种编码器实验显示ADR显著优于部署评分（后者即使微调后在多数方向均衡集上仍接近随机）；ADR优于所评估的MLLM似然readout，并在极小计算量下与MLLM chat推理竞争。

Conclusion: 方向信息仍存在于冻结编码器中，可通过合适的readout恢复。实现与评估工具将公开。

Abstract: CLIP-like vision-language models remain a cornerstone of multimodal systems, yet their scores stay near chance on directed spatial relations, such as whether one object is left of another. We call this failure readout blindness and analyze, theoretically and empirically, why deployed scores miss the direction: when scoring rules treat the subject and object symmetrically, direction cancels regardless of encoder training. Guided by this analysis, we introduce Antisymmetric Displacement Readout (ADR), which aligns caption words with image patches in the frozen features and scores each relation by the signed displacement between matched object centroids. Notably, ADR succeeds without additional training or learned parameters, thereby demonstrating that directional information remains in the frozen encoder. However, text and world priors can inflate accuracy, so we further introduce prior deflation, which measures the benefit of the image-text pairing as the grounded gain over a null that pairs each item with an unrelated image. Extensive experiments across encoder families show that ADR substantially improves over deployed scores, which remain near chance on most direction-balanced sets even for fine-tuned encoders. Compared with more complex readouts, ADR outperforms the evaluated MLLM likelihood readouts and is competitive with their chat inference at a small fraction of the computation. These results support our claim that directional information can be recovered from frozen features by an appropriate readout. Our implementation and evaluation kit will be publicly available.

</details>


### [194] [SPIN: Image Immunization Against Diffusion Editing via Single-Step Projection in Stochastic Neighborhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06334)
*Fengming Gu,Jie Zhang,Zhongqi Wang,Qiankun Li,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPIN框架，通过局部随机轨迹邻域上的单步投影实现图像免疫，无需完整轨迹反传即可有效抵御指令引导的图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型推动了指令引导的图像编辑，但带来未经授权篡改风险。图像免疫通过添加不可感知扰动来破坏后续编辑。由于编辑指令在图像发布时未知，保护需超越构造扰动所用指令。现有方法要么需昂贵的全轨迹反传，要么使用中间目标而效果被后续去噪削弱，且单条推理路径提供的关于其他去噪延续的反馈有限。

Method: SPIN从早期去噪状态出发，在同一指令下生成随机邻近状态，并通过单步投影预测其干净隐变量，无需完整展开。随后优化有界输入扰动，使这些预测与干净编辑参考的平均偏差最大化，从而破坏多种可能的编辑结果。

Result: 在两个图像编辑器上实验，SPIN在已见指令下六项指标全部超越对比方法，在更具挑战的未见指令设定下同样表现优异，保护性能显著提升。

Conclusion: SPIN以单步投影替代全轨迹反传，在降低计算成本的同时提升图像免疫的跨指令泛化保护效果。

Abstract: Diffusion models have greatly advanced instruction-guided image editing, while also raising concerns about unauthorized image manipulation. Image immunization addresses this risk by adding imperceptible perturbations to an input image to disrupt subsequent edits. Since editing requests are unknown at image release, protection should remain effective beyond the instruction used to construct the perturbation. Existing immunization methods either require costly full-trajectory backpropagation or use intermediate objectives whose effects may be weakened by subsequent denoising. Meanwhile, a single inference path provides limited feedback about alternative denoising continuations. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{SPIN}, a framework for image immunization via one-step projection over local stochastic trajectory neighborhoods. Starting from an early denoising state, \textsc{SPIN} generates stochastic neighboring states under the same instruction and predicts their clean latents through one-step projection without full unrolling. We then optimize a bounded input perturbation to maximize the average deviation of these predictions from a clean-edit reference, encouraging the perturbation to disrupt multiple possible editing outcomes. Experiments on two image editors demonstrate substantial gains in protection performance, with \textsc{SPIN} outperforming compared methods across all six metrics under seen instructions and in the more challenging unseen instruction setting.

</details>


### [195] [BabelFake: A Multilingual Audio-Visual DeepFake Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06339)
*Carlotta Segna,Joel Tschesche,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BabelFake，一个多语言、经同意的音视频DeepFake检测基准，包含399k片段（1,323小时），覆盖5种语言、496人，以及11种视频操纵方法与4种声音克隆引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频DeepFake数据集多限于英语、操纵类型过时、忽略音频模态，且常未经被使用者同意。

Method: 模块化数据生成管线，结合11种现代视频操纵方法与4种声音克隆引擎，区分纯视觉（换脸）与音视频联合（唇同步、肖像动画）操纵，并基于该基准评测SOTA检测器。

Result: 检测难度依音视频生成配对而变，保留真实音频时性能显著下降；跨语言/人口统计评估显示检测器敏感性因结构与训练数据而异；人类感知逼真度与机器检测难度并不一致。

Conclusion: BabelFake为多语言、经同意的音视频DeepFake检测提供更贴近现实的基准，揭示检测难点与感知-检测偏差。

Abstract: Reliable and practical audio-visual DeepFake detection requires benchmarks that reflect diverse linguistic contexts and modern data synthesis pipelines for visual as well as audio manipulations. However, existing datasets predominantly contain footage of English-speakers, often include outdated manipulation types, or overlook the audio modality. Further, many datasets feature individuals who did not consent to be used in DeepFake creation. We introduce BabelFake, a multilingual audio-visual DeepFake benchmark recorded with consenting participants. BabelFake contains 399k clips (1,323 hours) from 496 individuals spanning five languages (English, German, Italian, French, Spanish). Our modular data generation pipeline pairs 11 modern video manipulation methods with 4 voice cloning engines, distinguishing visual-only (face swapping) and joint audio-visual manipulations (lip synchronization and portrait animation). By benchmarking state-of-the-art detectors, we show that detection difficulty depends on the audio-visual generation pairing, with substantial performance degradation when authentic audio is preserved. Cross-language/demographic evaluation reveals sensitivity varying across detector architectures and training data, while human evaluation reveals that perceived realism and machine-detection difficulty do not necessarily align.

</details>


### [196] [MeSD: Multi-Evidence Self-Distillation for VideoLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06342)
*Weijie Zhu,Han Fang,Hanyu Fu,Yuzhe Zhang,Xin Wei,Zhaoyan Pan,Feiran Liu,Xunjie Jin,Hongbo Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Tianyi Gao,Tianyi Ding,Ye Yuan,Zhongjiang He,Hao Sun,Zhiheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MeSD多证据自蒸馏框架，用三个证据条件教师和验证引导优化为VideoLLM提供密集token级监督，提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励强化学习只提供序列级奖励，缺乏token级指导；单教师自蒸馏虽能提供密集监督，但混合异质证据会掩盖证据间的一致与冲突，且难以判断教师指导应用于修正奖励更新还是对失败轨迹提供纠正监督。

Method: 构建三个共享参数的证据条件教师（以GT答案为共同语义，分别加入时序和空间证据）；对相同学生前缀评估各证据相对Answer Teacher的偏好，融合教师共性偏好与门控教师特异残差；引入Verification-Guided Optimization将轨迹分为成功/失败/不确定，对成功和不确定轨迹细化token级优势幅度并保留奖励符号，对含所需证据的失败轨迹用reverse-KL向融合分布做纠正蒸馏。

Result: 在多个视频基准上相比强化学习与自蒸馏基线取得一致性增益。

Conclusion: MeSD通过多证据分解与验证引导优化，有效解决异质证据聚合与教师指导定位问题，为VideoLLM提供更可靠的token级监督。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards provides reliable outcome supervision for VideoLLMs, sequence-level rewards offer limited token-level guidance. On-policy self-distillation addresses this limitation by conditioning a self-teacher on privileged information to provide dense token-level supervision. However, aggregating heterogeneous evidence within a single teacher context obscures cross-evidence agreement and conflict. A further challenge lies in determining whether teacher guidance should refine reward-based updates or provide corrective supervision for failed trajectories. To address these issues, we propose MeSD, a multi-evidence self-distillation framework for VideoLLMs. MeSD constructs three evidence-conditioned teachers with shared parameters, using the ground-truth answer as a common semantic context while separately incorporating temporal and spatial evidence. Given the same student-generated prefixes, MeSD evaluates evidence-specific preferences relative to the Answer Teacher and fuses teacher-common preferences with gated teacher-specific residuals. Furthermore, MeSD introduces Verification-Guided Optimization to classify trajectories as Success, Failure, or Indeterminate. For Success and Indeterminate trajectories, MeSD refines token-level advantage magnitudes while preserving reward-derived signs. For verified failure trajectories that contain the required evidence, MeSD applies failure-conditioned distillation, using reverse-KL correction toward the fused distribution. Experiments on multiple video benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over reinforcement learning and self-distillation baselines.

</details>


### [197] [KineWorld: Action-Induced Transport Fields for Embodied World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06349)
*Ziying Song,Yuchen Liu,Zhuoran Xu,Ziyang Liu,Jian Jin,Jiangtao Su,Haibao Yu,Lei Yang,Yuanpei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KineWorld，将机器人运动学从运动条件扩展到生成监督的空间分配，通过传输感知的世界建模提升具身预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型采用均匀加权的视觉生成目标，与具身预测需求错位；即使有显式运动条件，也会低估对交互关键的空间稀疏变化。

Method: KineWorld框架包含两部分：Kinematic Transport Lifting (KTL)从指令机器人运动构建渲染器导出的相机对齐传输场；Transport-Aware World Diffusion (TAWD)在视频潜网格上校准运动支撑，并通过均匀分布与传输聚焦分布的归一化混合来重加权未来RGB流匹配。使用RoboTwin 2.0的ALOHA-AgileX双手操作数据训练。

Result: 单视图评估EWMScore-P达68.95，多视图评估TWB-Score达54.82。

Conclusion: 结果支持从外观拟合转向动作后果建模，服务具身决策。

Abstract: Embodied world models predict the visual consequences of candidate actions before execution. However, existing action-conditioned world models often adopt uniformly weighted visual generation objectives that can be misaligned with embodied prediction needs. Even with explicit motion conditioning, these objectives can underemphasize spatially sparse changes that are critical to interaction. We propose KineWorld, a transport-aware world-modeling framework that extends robot kinematics from motion conditioning to the spatial allocation of generative supervision. Kinematic Transport Lifting (KTL) constructs renderer-derived, camera-aligned transport fields from commanded robot motion. Transport-Aware World Diffusion (TAWD) calibrates their motion support on the video-latent grid and reweights future-RGB flow matching through a normalized mixture of uniform and transport-focused distributions. We train KineWorld using ALOHA-AgileX bimanual manipulation data from RoboTwin 2.0. KineWorld achieves an EWMScore-P of 68.95 in single-view evaluation and a TWB-Score of 54.82 in multi-view evaluation. These results support a shift from appearance fitting toward action-consequence modeling for embodied decision-making.

</details>


### [198] [Environmental sensor readings in two crop disease image datasets identify the session in which each image was taken](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06369)
*Sungwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示多模态作物病害分类中，环境传感器带来的精度提升常是数据构造导致的会话泄漏假象，而非真实增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究普遍报告融合环境传感器数据与叶片图像可提升作物病害分类精度，但作者怀疑这些增益可能源于数据集的会话泄漏问题。

Method: 分析两个广泛使用的韩国数据集（CDD基准和AI Hub病虫害数据集），检验图像与传感器值的共享情况，并构建仅依赖时间戳的"无图像"分类器进行对比。

Result: 几乎所有图像共享传感器值，91.9%的CDD测试图像在训练集中有完全相同的传感器值。仅用时间戳的无图像分类器在全部七种作物上达到或超过传感器驱动的预测，并匹敌已发表的SOTA CDD融合模型的macro-F1。

Conclusion: 标准随机划分上的性能增益无法与会话泄漏分离；多模态作物研究必须在会话留出划分上评估，并与无传感器的日期-时间基线对比以确保真实泛化。

Abstract: Integrating environmental sensor data with leaf imagery is widely reported to boost crop disease classification accuracy. In this work, we reveal that these reported gains are often artifacts of dataset construction: because a single sensor reading is shared across many images collected in a single session (one farm on one date), multimodal networks can predict disease simply by memorizing session identities. Analyzing two widely used Korean datasets, the Crop Disease Diagnosis (CDD) benchmark and an AI Hub pest/disease dataset, we demonstrate that nearly all images share sensor values, with 91.9% of CDD test images having exact sensor duplicates in the training set. Remarkably, an image-free classifier given only timestamps matches or exceeds sensor-driven predictions across all seven evaluated crops, and matches the published macro-F1 of a state-of-the-art CDD fusion model. These results indicate that performance gains on standard random splits cannot be disentangled from session leakage. We propose that multimodal crop studies must evaluate on session-held-out splits and report performance against sensor-free date-time baselines to ensure genuine generalization.

</details>


### [199] [MTOR: Generalizable AI-Generated Video Detection with Multimodal Semantics and Temporal Over-Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06378)
*Hang Wang,Chao Shen,Lei Zhang,Zhi-Qi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MTOR框架，结合字幕文本语义与时间过规律性(TOR)检测AI生成视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器主要依赖视觉表示，忽视了字幕派生的文本语义，同时对细粒度视觉表示中的时间规律性关注有限。作者发现文本表示能提供互补判别线索，且AI生成视频呈现更强的时间持续性和更低的时间变异性（即TOR）。

Method: MTOR包含多模态分支和TOR组件：多模态分支整合全局视觉表示与字幕文本表示；TOR组件在三个层次建模时间过规律性——粗粒度帧间连续性、细粒度token对应、帧到视频稳定性。

Result: 在覆盖46种生成器变体的五个基准上评估，整体性能优于16个代表性基线达到SOTA，并对12种真实世界视频扰动展现强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 字幕文本语义与时间过规律性是AI生成视频检测的有效互补线索，MTOR实现领先性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of video generation has narrowed the perceptual gap between authentic and synthetic videos, making generalizable AI-generated video detection increasingly challenging. Existing detectors predominantly rely on visual representations, leaving caption-derived textual semantics underexplored. Meanwhile, temporal regularity in fine-grained visual representations has received limited attention. We find that caption-derived textual representations provide complementary discriminative cues to global visual representations. Our analysis further reveals that AI-generated videos exhibit stronger temporal persistence and lower temporal variability, a pattern we term temporal over-regularity (TOR). Based on these findings, we propose MTOR with a multimodal branch and a TOR component. The multimodal branch integrates global visual and caption-derived textual representations, while the TOR component models temporal over-regularity at three levels: coarse inter-frame continuity, fine-grained token correspondence, and frame-to-video stability. Extensive evaluations on five benchmarks covering 46 generator variants demonstrate state-of-the-art overall performance against 16 representative baselines, while robustness experiments confirm strong resilience to twelve real-world video perturbations. Code and models will be released at https://github.com/hwang-cs-ime/MTOR.

</details>


### [200] [Multi-Task Partially Supervised Learning for Super-Resolution and Semantic Segmentation on Earth Observation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06389)
*Hoàng-Ân Lê,Minh-Tan Pham,Solange Lemai-Chenevier,Daniel Greslou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究超分辨率与语义分割的多任务部分监督学习：每个样本仅有一个任务标注。提出混合架构变体与重投影损失，实验优于SOTA顺序变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超分辨率与语义分割互相促进，尤其在遥感场景。但联合训练通常需要两个任务的标注，成本高且不现实。

Method: 研究多任务部分监督范式（每个样本仅有单任务标注），考察顺序与共享两种架构变体，并提出混合变体和重投影损失，以利用共享表示并在语义分割训练时保证超分辨率图像质量。

Result: 实验相比SOTA顺序变体取得更好结果。代码将发布于 https://github.com/lhoangan/munera。

Conclusion: 混合架构结合重投影损失可在部分监督下有效联合学习超分辨率与语义分割。

Abstract: Super-resolution and semantic segmentation are known to benefit one another, especially in the Earth observation context. However, learning both tasks in a joint model often requires both task annotations, which is impractical and expensive. In this paper, we study the multi-task partially supervised learning paradigm for both tasks, where each example is assumed to have only a single-task annotation. To that end, we examine two multi-task architectural variations, the sequential and shared variants, and then propose a hybrid variant and a re-projection loss to benefit from the shared representation and enforce image quality of super-resolution when training with semantic segmentation. Experiments show favorable results compared to the SOTA sequential variant. Source code will be published at https://github.com/lhoangan/munera.

</details>


### [201] [Harnessing Multimodal Large Language Models for Training-Free Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06394)
*Zhaolin Cai,Huiyu Duan,Liu Yang,Yanjun Qin,Bo Ai,Wei Chen,Xiongkuo Min,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练HOI检测框架HarnessHOI，通过交互引导感知和关系无关的几何裁定解决MLLM推理碎片化与语义循环问题，在HICO-DET和V-COCO上达到免训练方法SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督HOI检测依赖任务特定训练；现有免训练MLLM方法推理阶段松散协调，交互假设无法引导视觉探索，导致参与者被忽略、局部歧义未解，且早期语义假设在后续视觉定位中传播形成自我强化的语义循环。

Method: 将被动MLLM推理转为主动的以交互为中心的执行框架：1）交互引导感知机制，将新兴交互假设投影回视觉空间以发现遗漏参与者并通过定向观察精炼歧义证据；2）关系无关的几何裁定模块，协调多源证据建立统一空间基准，支持多动作与语义角色的接地交互推理。

Result: 在HICO-DET和V-COCO上的大量实验表明，HarnessHOI在免训练方法中取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效缓解推理碎片化与语义循环，验证了面向复杂交互理解的有效性，代码将于发表后开源。

Abstract: Human-object interaction (HOI) detection aims to localize human-object pairs and recognize their interactions. Traditional supervised methods perform strongly but rely on task-specific training. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer a promising route to training-free HOI detection through their broad visual-semantic knowledge and versatile perceptual and reasoning capabilities. However, existing approaches largely invoke these capabilities through loosely coordinated inference stages. This fragmented execution restricts the role of interaction hypotheses in guiding visual exploration, leaving key participants overlooked and local ambiguities unresolved. Furthermore, propagating early semantic assumptions through subsequent visual grounding and relation prediction induces self-reinforcing semantic circularity. To resolve these challenges, we propose HarnessHOI, a training-free framework that transforms passive MLLM inference into an active interaction-centric harness. Specifically, we introduce an interaction-guided perception mechanism that projects emerging interaction hypotheses back into the visual space to discover missing participants and refine ambiguous evidence through targeted observation. Furthermore, a relation-agnostic geometric adjudication module reconciles multi-source evidence to establish a unified spatial basis for grounded interaction reasoning across multiple actions and semantic roles. Extensive experiments on HICO-DET and V-COCO demonstrate that HarnessHOI achieves state-of-the-art performance among training-free methods, confirming the effectiveness of the proposed harness for complex interaction understanding. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [202] [SpatialChain: A Benchmark for Auditing Spatial Reasoning Faithfulness in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06413)
*Rafael Teixeira Sousa,Vinícius Paulo Lopes de Oliveira,Elisa Ayumi Masasi de Oliveira,Luiza Martins de Freitas Cintra,Fernanda Bufon Färber,Igor Dias Aguiar,Julia Yasmim de Almeida Nobre,Arlindo Rodrigues Galvão Filho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpatialChain 数据集与双轴评估协议，揭示 thinking-enabled VLM 在空间基准上的"捷径"现象：高分不代表忠实推理，并证明用其数据微调可提升准确率并降低捷径率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 thinking-enabled VLM 在空间基准上的最终答案准确率无法区分"忠实空间推理"与"语言捷径"，即模型可能是靠语言先验猜对而非真正推理。

Method: 构建 SpatialChain：28,350 训练 + 899 测试样本，将 GQA 空间问题与场景图接地的推理链配对（仅保留答案与符号真值一致者）；设计双轴评估——客观链重叠指标 + 场景图感知的 LLM 裁判，独立于最终答案打分忠实度与完整性。

Result: 在 9 个模型上：(i) 4/9 模型 VQA 准确率≥79% 但捷径率>39%；(ii) 链质量对 7/9 模型显著预测答案正确性，两个例外（Claude Sonnet 4.6、InternVL3.5-8B）呈"过简输出 vs 冗长装饰性推理"两种失败模式；(iii) SFT 使 Qwen3-VL-8B 域内 +6.2pp、捷径率降至 22%，但外部基准出现风格特化效应，需 replay 增强缓解。裁判经 198 条人工标注验证（人机一致性≈人人一致性），并与另一家供应商裁判一致（ρ=0.88）。

Conclusion: 仅看准确率会掩盖 VLM 的空间推理缺陷；SpatialChain 提供了可验证的推理链数据与忠实度评估方法，可用于诊断与改善模型的真实空间推理能力。

Abstract: Thinking-enabled vision-language models (VLMs) report ever-higher accuracy on spatial benchmarks, yet final-answer scores cannot reveal whether a correct prediction reflects faithful spatial reasoning or a linguistic shortcut. We introduce SpatialChain, a dataset of 28,350 training and 899 test examples pairing spatially-oriented GQA questions with scene-graph-grounded reasoning chains, retained only when the generated answer matches the symbolic ground truth, and a two-axis evaluation combining objective chain-overlap metrics with a scene-graph-aware LLM judge that scores faithfulness and completeness independently of the final answer. Applied to nine thinking-enabled VLMs, the protocol surfaces three findings invisible to standard accuracy: (i) four of nine models achieve $\geq$79% VQA accuracy while exhibiting shortcut rates above 39%, i.e., correct answers whose reasoning the judge marks as unfaithful; (ii) chain quality significantly predicts answer correctness for seven of nine models, but the two exceptions (Claude Sonnet 4.6, InternVL3.5-8B) reveal qualitatively distinct failure modes, terse output vs. verbose-decorative reasoning, that benchmark accuracy alone conflates; (iii) SFT on SpatialChain improves Qwen3-VL-8B by +6.2 pp in-domain and reduces its shortcut rate to 22%, while a stylistic specialization effect on external benchmarks motivates replay-augmented training as mitigation. The faithfulness judge is validated against 198 human-annotated items, where judge-human agreement matches human-human agreement, and against a second judge from a different provider, which preserves the model ranking ($ρ$ = 0.88). Data, generation scripts, and evaluation code are released at https://github.com/spatialchain/SpatialChainBenchmark.

</details>


### [203] [Toward Reliable Infant Pose Estimation: A Training-Dynamics Approach to Noisy Annotation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06423)
*Emanuele Cardinale,Sara Moccia,Alessandro Cacciatore,Lucia Migliorelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于训练动力学的噪声关键点检测框架，用于早产儿姿态估计，F1达91.9%，并在COCO上提升下游精度最高7.4 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早产儿运动分析依赖无标记姿态估计，但训练准确模型需大量人工标注关键点，而人工标注易出错；噪声关键点（相对真实解剖位置错位）会污染重建的关节轨迹，产生伪影，故需检测噪声标注。

Method: 基于小损失假设与噪声标签学习的训练动力学样本选择：训练混合卷积-注意力模型，从关键点空间坐标与局部视觉特征预测其解剖类别；利用交叉熵训练动力学导出每关键点描述子，再通过无监督聚类划分为干净与噪声子集。

Result: 在新建的65例住院早产儿数据集NeoPose上，两种噪声场景（随机位置扰动、左右交换）多种噪声水平下，噪声关键点检测F1最高91.9%；在异构COCO基准上，过滤检测到的噪声关键点后，中高噪声水平下下游姿态估计精度提升最高7.4 AP。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效检测关键点标注噪声，并泛化到通用基准，通过过滤噪声样本提升下游姿态估计性能，具有临床应用价值。

Abstract: Spontaneous movement analysis in preterm infants relies increasingly on markerless pose estimation (PE) to derive clinically relevant motion biomarkers directly from video recordings. Training accurate infant PE models requires large sets of manually annotated keypoints, and human annotation is inherently prone to error. Noisy keypoints (i.e., keypoints mislocalized with respect to their true anatomical position) are especially problematic in this clinical setting, since they can propagate as artificial artifacts into the reconstructed joint trajectories. Building on the small-loss hypothesis and training-dynamics-based sample selection established in the noisy-label learning literature, we propose a novel framework for detecting noisy keypoint annotations. A hybrid convolutional-attention model is trained to predict the anatomical category of each keypoint from its spatial coordinates and local visual features; the resulting cross-entropy training dynamics are then used to derive per-keypoint descriptors, which are partitioned into clean and noisy subsets via unsupervised clustering. We validate the approach on NeoPose, a newly collected dataset of 65 hospitalized preterm infants, under two realistic noise scenarios (random positional perturbation and left-right swapping) across multiple noise levels. Results show that the proposed approach achieves an F1-score of up to 91.9% in noisy-keypoint detection. The framework further generalizes to the heterogeneous COCO benchmark, where filtering CE-detected noisy keypoints from the training set also yields measurable improvements (up to 7.4 AP points) in downstream pose estimation accuracy at moderate-to-high noise levels.

</details>


### [204] [MaRO-GS: Mask-Robust Object-Centric Gaussian Splatting from Inconsistent Multi-view Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06472)
*Eunji Kim,Gahyeon Kim,Gianella Cravioto,Dong-hun Lee,Chaewon Moon,Chae-yeong Song,Sang-hyo Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MaRO-GS框架，直接从对象掩码多视图图像优化目标物体高斯，鲁棒处理不一致监督，提升重建质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对象级3DGS方法重建整个场景而非直接优化目标物体，计算开销大；且依赖跨视图不一致的2D分割掩码，导致高斯优化被污染、产生错误监督，降低重建保真度。

Method: MaRO-GS直接优化目标物体高斯并鲁棒应对不一致监督，包含三项设计：掩码可靠性视图过滤排除不可靠视图；对象支撑高斯密度控制抑制无关高斯并防止背景密集化；轮廓对齐对象损失保持面向对象的优化。

Result: 在多个数据集上实验证明，MaRO-GS提升PSNR、分割精度与计算效率，在小型物体LERF-Mask数据集上PSNR最大增益达2.05 dB。

Conclusion: MaRO-GS通过直接优化目标物体高斯并对抗不一致监督，实现更高质量、更高效的对象级3D重建。

Abstract: We address the challenge of accurate 3D object reconstruction from multi-view images in Gaussian Splatting. Existing object-level 3DGS methods reconstruct the entire scene rather than directly optimizing the target object, even when only the target object is needed, which incurs substantial computational overhead. They also rely on 2D segmentation masks to associate Gaussians with objects, but these masks are often inconsistent across views. Such inconsistencies corrupt Gaussian optimization and produce incorrectly supervised Gaussians that degrade object reconstruction fidelity. To overcome these limitations, we propose MaRO-GS, a 3DGS framework that directly optimizes target-object Gaussians from object-masked multi-view images and remains robust to inconsistent supervision. For reliable supervision, mask-reliability view filtering excludes unreliable views. Object-supported Gaussian density control suppresses Gaussians irrelevant to the target object and prevents background densification, while Silhouette-aligned Object Loss maintains object-focused optimization. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that MaRO-GS improves PSNR, segmentation accuracy, and computational efficiency, with the largest PSNR gain of 2.05 dB on the small-object LERF-Mask dataset.

</details>


### [205] [Topology-Informed Prompt-Conditioned Universal Segmentation of Uterine Structures from Ultrasound and MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06494)
*Yongheng Sun,Yuexi Gu,Jingwen Sun,Maureen Kohi,Mingxia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TPUS框架，用图结构骨干+任务提示统一分割超声与MRI的多种子宫结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子宫多结构分割对疾病筛查、诊断和治疗规划很重要，超声与MRI信息互补；但两种模态在图像外观、解剖背景、空间分辨率和标签空间上差异大，且现有数据集分割目标不一致，导致跨模态联合学习困难并可能产生负迁移。

Method: TPUS包含：基于图的多数据集骨干（模态专属stem+模态共享图编解码器）实现模态敏感输入适配、结构特征推理与联合表示学习；任务感知类别提示（task-aware class prompts）适配不同数据集和标签空间；动态卷积适配模块生成任务特定输出响应；拓扑感知损失保证解剖一致性。

Result: 在子宫超声数据集和T2加权子宫肌瘤MRI数据集的留出测试集上，Dice分别为0.898和0.693，优于多种通用与统一分割基线。代码已开源。

Conclusion: TPUS能有效统一跨超声与MRI的异构子宫分割任务，通过拓扑先验与提示条件化缓解负迁移，实现较优分割性能。

Abstract: Multi-structure segmentation of the uterus is important for computer-assisted screening, diagnosis, and treatment planning of uterine diseases, where ultrasound and MRI provide complementary clinical information. However, developing a unified model across these modalities is challenging due to their substantially different image appearances, anatomical contexts, spatial resolutions, and label spaces. Moreover, existing datasets often define different segmentation targets, making joint learning challenging and potentially leading to negative transfer across heterogeneous tasks. To this end, we propose a Topology-informed Prompt-conditioned Universal Segmentation (TPUS) framework for segmenting multiple uterine structures across ultrasound and MRI. TPUS introduces a graph-based multi-dataset backbone comprising modality-specific stems and a modality-shared graph-based encoder-decoder to support modality-sensitive input adaptation, structural feature reasoning, and joint representation learning across heterogeneous uterine segmentation tasks. In addition, TPUS uses task-aware class prompts to condition the segmentation process for different datasets and label spaces, a dynamic convolutional adaptation module to generate task-specific output responses, and a topology-informed loss to encourage anatomically consistent predictions. Experiments on a uterine ultrasound dataset and a T2-weighted uterine myoma MRI dataset demonstrate that TPUS achieves Dice scores of 0.898 and 0.693 on the two held-out test sets, respectively, outperforming several generic and universal segmentation baselines. Source code can be accessed at https://github.com/YonghengSun1997/TPUS.

</details>


### [206] [NeuroCBIR: A Fast and Accurate Image Retrieval System for Whole-Brain and Region-Specific MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06502)
*Felix Nieto-del-Amor,Jingru Fu,J. -Sebastian Muehlboeck,Eric Westman,Daniel Ferreira,Rodrigo Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuroCBIR 是一个用于全脑及脑区级 3D T1w MRI 快速检索的框架，基于 VAE 与对比学习生成嵌入，支持可复现研究并公开 26,000+ 预计算嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经影像 CBIR 方法多受限于小数据集、单一脑区或粗粒度类标签，临床实用性与泛化性不足。

Method: 提取 103 个皮层与皮层下脑区；用变分自编码器(VAE)的潜在表示结合对比学习，生成捕获解剖模式的扫描级嵌入，支持全脑与脑区级查询。

Result: 受试者重识别在全脑和脑区级均表现优异(mAP@5 ≥ 98.4%)，跨数据集和采集条件泛化稳健；零样本年龄预测与多类病理分层表明嵌入含下游任务有用信息；4 核 CPU 上嵌入提取约 18.7 秒/扫描，相似性检索 <0.01 秒。

Conclusion: NeuroCBIR 支持区域灵活检索与可复现研究，可提供临床意义的个性化诊断支持，软件及 26,000+ T1w 嵌入已公开。

Abstract: Content-based image retrieval (CBIR) in neuroimaging enables the identification of structurally similar brain scans, supporting diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment planning; however, existing methods are often limited to small datasets, single brain regions, or coarse class labels, thereby restricting their clinical utility and generalizability.
  Here, we present NeuroCBIR, a framework for fast and flexible retrieval of both whole-brain and region-specific 3D T1w MRI scans. A total of 103 cortical and subcortical regions are extracted to enable both whole-brain and region-level queries.
  NeuroCBIR leverages latent representations learned by a variational autoencoder (VAE) combined with contrastive learning, producing scan-specific embeddings that capture anatomical patterns. These embeddings were evaluated for subject re-identification, zero-shot age prediction, and zero-shot multi-class pathology stratification. Re-identification performance was high across both whole-brain and brain-region levels (mean average precision across the top-5 retrieved images (mAP@5) >= 98.4%), with robust generalization across datasets and acquisition conditions. While NeuroCBIR is not trained for age prediction or pathology stratification, zero-shot evaluations for these two tasks demonstrate that the embeddings encode meaningful information for downstream tasks.
  Embedding extraction on a 4-core CPU required approximately 18.7 s per scan, whereas similarity search was effectively instantaneous (less than 0.01 s).
  NeuroCBIR is publicly available for brain MRI with more than 26,000 precomputed T1w MRI embeddings. It supports reproducible research, region-specific flexibility, and clinically meaningful personalized diagnostic support. The software is available at https://github.com/minnelab/NeuroCBIR.

</details>


### [207] [Harmful Content Generation in Text-to-Image Models: Capabilities and Moderation Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06503)
*Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Manos Schinas,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统评估五个开源文生图模型的有害内容生成能力，发现尽管有安全机制，有害图像生成率仍很高，且审核与检测系统存在明显漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图模型能生成逼真图像但引发滥用担忧，现有安全机制的有效性缺乏针对真实攻击的系统评估。

Method: 构建自动化流程，将合法新闻标题转化为针对色情、暴力/血腥、有害刻板印象、自残和仇恨言论的不安全提示，评估五个开源模型（含内置安全机制的模型及绕过限制的社区微调变体），对1,500张生成图像进行人工评估，并测试自动审核系统与合成图像检测器。

Result: 有害内容生成率高：血腥89.2%、色情47.6%、刻板印象43.6%、仇恨言论46.0%、自残34.5%；社区微调模型对色情提示尤其脆弱；FLUX.1-dev在30.9%情况下生成明显逼真有害图像；审核系统存在检测缺口；仅用良性数据训练的检测器对色情内容表现更差。

Conclusion: 现有生成安全机制、审核系统和合成图像检测均存在局限，需更强防护应对大规模滥用。

Abstract: Text-to-image generative models can produce highly realistic imagery but also raise concerns about harmful misuse. While safety mechanisms exist, systematic evaluations of their effectiveness against realistic attacks remain limited. We present a systematic evaluation of harmful content generation across five open text-to-image models using an automated pipeline that transforms legitimate news captions into unsafe prompts targeting sexually explicit content, violence/gore, harmful stereotypes, self-harm, and hate speech. We evaluate both standard models with built-in safety mechanisms and community fine-tuned variants that bypass content restrictions. A human evaluation of 1,500 generated images shows high harmful-content generation rates: 89.2% for gore-related prompts, 47.6% for sexually explicit content, 43.6% for harmful stereotypes, 46.0% for hate speech, and 34.5% for self-harm, predominantly through graphic violence. Models show substantial capability for generating violent and stereotypical content, while community fine-tuned variants are particularly vulnerable to sexually explicit prompts. Generation quality is largely preserved under harmful prompting, producing imagery of sufficient fidelity to pose risks for disinformation and abuse; FLUX.1-dev produces clearly realistic harmful images in 30.9% of cases. We further evaluate automated moderation systems and find substantial detection gaps that allow unsafe images to evade filtering. Finally, we assess synthetic image detectors and show that models trained only on benign datasets perform worse on explicit content, while more diverse training data improves detection, highlighting semantic distribution gaps in current approaches. These findings expose limitations in current generation safeguards, moderation systems, and synthetic image detection, highlighting the need for stronger defenses against misuse at scale.

</details>


### [208] [A General Pipeline for Dense Illuminant Estimation via Physically Based Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06508)
*Luca Cogo,Gianmarco Corti,Simone Bianco,Raimondo Schettini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可从3D渲染场景生成稠密光源色度图的通用流程，构建74,321张合成图像用于预训练光源估计模型，单光源估计提升最高28%，多光源最高57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光源估计是计算摄影的基础问题，用于校正光照导致的色彩偏移。基于学习的方法性能强，但受限于缺乏大规模且具有准确光源真值的数据集。

Method: 复用现有3D场景集合，通过基于物理的3D渲染流程，在受控光照条件下系统性生成像素级光源标注，得到稠密光源色度图，并据此生成74,321张大规模合成图像用于预训练。

Result: 用该合成数据预训练单光源与多光源估计模型，在多种先进架构上实验显示性能一致提升：单光源估计最高提升28%，多光源最高提升57%，在数据稀缺场景下尤为明显。

Conclusion: 合成数据生成流程为光源估计方法的预训练提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案，降低了数据获取门槛。

Abstract: Illuminant estimation is a fundamental problem in computational photography, as it enables the correction of color shifts induced by varying lighting conditions. While learning-based methods have demonstrated strong performance, their progress is hindered by the limited availability of large-scale datasets with accurate illuminant ground-truth. In this work, we propose a general and reusable pipeline to derive dense illuminant chromaticity maps from physically based 3D-rendered scenes. By repurposing an existing 3D scene collection, our approach enables the systematic generation of pixel-wise illuminant annotations under controlled lighting conditions, effectively lowering the barrier to data acquisition for learning-based illuminant estimation. Using this pipeline, we generate a large-scale synthetic set of 74,321 images, which we employ for pre-training both single- and multi-illuminant estimation models. Extensive experiments with state-of-the-art architectures show that synthetic pre-training consistently improves performance, with gains of up to 28% for single-illuminant estimation and up to 57% for multi-illuminant estimation, particularly in data-scarce regimes. These findings demonstrate that synthetic data generation pipelines offer an effective and scalable solution for the pre-training of illuminant estimation methods.

</details>


### [209] [BrainTRACE: Tracing Longitudinal, Multimodal, and Volumetric Evidence in Brain MRI Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06571)
*Qizhen Lan,Mengchen Fan,Hang Zhang,Jingwei Duan,Moule Lin,Jialin Chen,Baocheng Geng,Xiaoqian Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainTRACE 是一个报告落地的脑 MRI 纵向解读基准，含 7,273 个 VQA 实例、1,778 名纵向患者与约 29k 个 3D 序列体数据，按五级临床推理与四类证据需求组织，评估 20 种 VLM 配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑 MRI 解读本质是纵向临床推理：需比较序列研究、整合多序列、在容积解剖中定位，并转化为报告级评估。现有医学 VQA 与 3D 影像基准多以孤立图像、静态体数据或无依据的报告式答案评估，掩盖了证据链中的失效，难以反映临床有效性。

Method: 构建 BrainTRACE 基准，源自 1,778 名纵向患者、7,299 次 MRI 研究、约 29k 个共配准 3D 序列体数据；含 7,273 个带评分 VQA。按五级临床推理（从采集识别到病例级综合）和四类证据需求（纵向比较、报告落地引用、多序列整合、容积空间证据）组织。支持标准 VLM 渲染输入、3D 证据条件，以及审计纵向证据链六步的分解式病例推理轨道。

Result: 评估 20 种 VLM 配置显示，当前系统能识别孤立视觉线索，但很少将其组合成有依据的纵向解读。

Conclusion: 发布基准规范、评估列表、评分实现、评分标准与审计记录格式，以支持脑 MRI VLM 评估的可复现进展。

Abstract: Brain MRI interpretation is a longitudinal clinical reasoning problem: radiologists compare serial studies, integrate information across MRI sequences, localize findings within volumetric anatomy, and translate this evidence into report-grounded assessments. Existing medical VQA and 3D imaging benchmarks capture important parts of this workflow, but often evaluate brain MRI through isolated images, static volumes, or ungrounded report-style answers, thereby obscuring failures in the evidence chain that support clinical validity. We introduce BrainTRACE, a report-grounded benchmark for evaluating whether vision-language models can trace the evidence structure required for longitudinal brain MRI interpretation. BrainTRACE contains 7,273 scored VQA instances derived from 1,778 longitudinal patients, 7,299 MRI studies, and approximately 29k co-registered 3D MRI sequence volumes. The benchmark is organized by five levels of clinical reasoning, from acquisition recognition to case-level synthesis, and by evidence demands covering longitudinal comparison, report-grounded references, multi-sequence integration, and volumetric spatial evidence. BrainTRACE supports rendered inputs compatible with standard VLM interfaces, a 3D-evidence condition, and a decomposed case-reasoning track that audits six steps in a longitudinal evidence chain. Evaluation of 20 VLM configurations shows that current systems can identify isolated visual cues but rarely compose them into grounded longitudinal interpretations. We release the benchmark specification, evaluation lists, scoring implementation, scoring rubrics, and audit-record format to support reproducible progress in brain MRI VLM evaluation.

</details>


### [210] [FrontVeg V2: A Training-Free Software Framework for Foreground-Aware Zero-Shot Plant Trait Segmentation in High-Resolution Images of Trellised Crops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06575)
*Abdoul Djalil Ousseini Hamza,Herearii Metuarea,Corentin Lothod{é},Morgane Roth,Jacem Ben Hamden,Eric Duch{ê}ne,Lionel Ley,David Alletru,David Rousseau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrontVeg V2 是一个开源、免训练的前景感知零样本分割框架，用于高分辨率篱架作物图像中植物性状分割，无需任务特定重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率篱架作物图像中，相邻植被行常导致误检测，影响植物器官和病害症状的准确分割；同时任务特定模型重训成本高。因此需要一个免训练、前景感知的通用框架。

Method: 整合单目深度估计、基于 Valley-Aware Depth Thresholding 的自动前景提取、分块零样本分割、基于图的掩膜组装以及几何感知融合；实现中集成 Depth Anything V2 (DAV2) 与 SAM3，支持命令行批处理和 Napari 图形界面。

Result: 可分割植物器官和病害症状，同时减少来自相邻植被行的误检测；支持多作物、多性状表型分析，无需任务特定模型重训。

Conclusion: FrontVeg V2 提供了一个可复用的数字表型框架，通过免训练的前景感知零样本分割实现多作物、多性状应用。

Abstract: FrontVeg V2 is an open-source, training-free software framework for foregroundaware zero-shot segmentation of plant traits in high-resolution images of trellised crops. The pipeline combines monocular depth estimation, automatic foreground extraction using Valley-Aware Depth Thresholding, tiled zero-shot segmentation, Graph-Based Mask Assembly, and geometry-aware fusion. This design enables plant organs and disease symptoms to be segmented while reducing detections arising from neighboring vegetation rows. The current implementation integrates Depth Anything V2 (DAV2) and SAM3 and can be used through both command-line batch processing and a Napari graphical interface. FrontVeg V2 provides a reusable framework for multi-crop, multi-trait digital phenotyping without task-specific model retraining.

</details>


### [211] [Keepsake: Selective Spatial Memory for Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06588)
*Abdul Mohaimen Al Radi,Kunyang Li,Yuzhang Shang,Mubarak Shah,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Keepsake，一个在线免训练的空间记忆控制器，用"关系型价值"动态决定固定容量记忆中哪些观测应保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程相机控制视频生成依赖持久记忆维持场景一致性。全历史方法存储和检索成本无界；选择性构建方法一次性决策且不再更新，无法适应观测价值随记忆库演化而变化的问题。核心问题：历史演进中，哪些存储观测仍应留在记忆中？

Method: 关键洞察是存储观测的价值是"关系型"的，取决于当前记忆库中的替代品。Keepsake在每次更新时，对保留和新生成观测构建"姿态-外观图"，结合相机姿态邻近度与视觉相似度。保留优先级同时考虑强替代品数量与最近替代品相似度，从而持续重估记忆价值：保护替代支持少的观测，在固定预算内淘汰高度可替代的观测。该方法仅修改持久记忆更新，主机生成器、去噪调度与检索规则均不变。

Result: 在MemCam与WorldMem上，固定记忆预算下Keepsake改善了FVD与LPIPS。在180秒MemCam轨迹上，仅保留5,397帧中的32帧，FVD降低35.1%。

Conclusion: 记忆价值应动态、关系化地评估；Keepsake以在线、免训练方式在固定容量下显著提升长时程视频生成一致性与质量。

Abstract: Long-horizon camera-controlled video generation relies on persistent memory to maintain scene consistency. Existing systems follow two strategies to achieve this consistency. Full-history approaches retain all generated observations, causing unbounded storage and retrieval costs. Selective-construction approaches reduce redundancy, but make one-time retention decisions that are never revisited, even as an observation's value changes with the evolving memory bank. Both strategies leave a shared question unresolved: as the generated history evolves, which stored observations should still remain in memory? Our key insight is that the value of a stored observation is not fixed, but relational: it depends on the alternatives currently available in the memory bank. A view supported by many geometrically and visually similar substitutes can be relinquished with little loss of coverage, whereas an observation with few viable alternatives should remain regardless of age. We introduce Keepsake, an online, training-free controller for fixed-capacity spatial memory. At each update, Keepsake constructs a pose-appearance graph over retained and newly generated observations, combining camera-pose proximity with visual similarity. A retention priority jointly captures the number of strong substitutes and the similarity of the closest alternative, allowing Keepsake to continually reassess memory value, preserve observations with little alternative support, and evict highly replaceable ones under a fixed budget. The controller modifies only the persistent-memory update; the host generator, denoising schedule, and retrieval rule remain unchanged. Across MemCam and WorldMem, Keepsake improves FVD and LPIPS under a fixed memory budget. On 180-second MemCam trajectories, it retains only 32 of 5,397 frames while reducing FVD by 35.1%.

</details>


### [212] [VGGT-Bridge: Beyond Sequential Pose Graphs via Coarse-Stride Skip Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06594)
*Sungjae Choi,Hanna Bae,Sunghyun Baek,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGT-Bridge通过引入长距离跳连边，将非相邻块直接约束，解决chunk-and-align框架中的序列漂移问题，无需重训练即显著降低ATE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈视觉几何transformer（如VGGT）因二次注意力复杂度难以扩展到数千帧长序列；现有chunk-and-align方法只连接顺序相邻块，导致逐帧小误差沿链累积成大尺度漂移。

Method: 提出VGGT-Bridge，添加长距离跳连边约束非相邻块（无需重训练）；对稀疏采样的粗略块运行VGGT，使每个粗略块桥接远端细块形成直接约束；利用VGGT首帧尺度偏差，将选定粗略块反向输入以进行漂移校正；采用loop-aware策略使反向操作兼容现有回环检测。

Result: 相比SwiftVGGT基线，ATE在KITTI Odometry上降低28.3%，Virtual KITTI上降低18.8%，Waymo Open上降低10.0%，在所有chunk-and-align方法中达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 通过长距离跳连而非仅顺序连接，可有效抑制长序列3D重建漂移，且无需重训练即实现当前最优的chunk-and-align性能。

Abstract: Feed-forward visual geometry transformers such as VGGT reconstruct dense 3D structure from images in a single forward pass, simplifying multi-view 3D reconstruction. However, their quadratic attention complexity makes them difficult to scale to long sequences with thousands of frames. Chunk-and-align frameworks address this by splitting a long sequence into overlapping chunks and stitching their local reconstructions into a pose graph. Yet existing methods connect only sequentially adjacent chunks, so small per-frame errors accumulate along the chain into large-scale drift. To move beyond sequential edges, we propose VGGT-Bridge, which adds long-range skip edges that directly constrain non-adjacent chunks without retraining. By running VGGT on sparsely sampled coarse chunks, each coarse chunk bridges distant fine chunks into a single direct constraint. We further turn VGGT's first-frame scale bias into a drift correction by feeding selected coarse chunks in reverse, and a loop-aware policy keeps this reversal compatible with existing loop closures. VGGT-Bridge reduces ATE by 28.3% on KITTI Odometry, 18.8% on Virtual KITTI, and 10.0% on Waymo Open over the SwiftVGGT baseline, achieving the best performance among all chunk-and-align methods.

</details>


### [213] [Analysis of SWIR Imaging Detection Performance Under Adverse Environmental Conditions for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06596)
*Rohan Mehra,Alexandre Riffard,Yannis Loumouamou,Mathieu Labussière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统对比RGB与短波红外(SWIR)在自动驾驶目标检测中的表现，发现RGB多数场景更优，SWIR在四类安全关键场景中具互补优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWIR成像被视为自动驾驶的潜力模态，但其相对RGB的实际优势在不同条件下缺乏系统刻画，需明确其适用边界。

Method: 在RASMD数据集上进行成对RGB-SWIR目标检测对比，覆盖四种天气条件与两种实时检测架构，采用统一真值进行多种微调评估；并提出"传感器优势挖掘"框架，结合多模型一致性与定向人工检查，在基本无标注的成对数据中定位某模态更可靠的场景。

Result: RGB在多数场景中表现相当或更优，RF-DETR在不同条件下更稳健；SWIR在四类安全关键场景具明显优势：挡风玻璃眩光、玻璃水珠、低对比度目标可见性、远距离车辆检测。

Conclusion: SWIR应被视为互补模态，用于在罕见但具挑战性的条件下增强感知；数据集可应请求获取，代码与模型权重已公开。

Abstract: Short-wave infrared (SWIR) imaging has emerged as a promising modality for autonomous driving, yet its practical benefits over RGB remain poorly characterized across diverse conditions. This paper presents a systematic comparative study of paired RGB and SWIR object detection on the RASMD dataset, covering four weather conditions and two real-time detection architectures, with various fine-tunings evaluated against a unified ground truth.
